			}
		}

			is_great_war_ongoing = yes

				NOT = {
					tag = ENG
					tag = SOV
				}
				is_major = yes
				has_capitulated = no
			}
			OR = {
				USA = {
					has_war_with = PREV
					has_capitulated = no
				}
				ENG = {
					has_war_with = PREV
					has_capitulated = no
				}	
			}
			has_war_support < 0.6
			OR = {
				has_war_support < 0.4
				has_idea = GER_famine
			}
			longest_war_length > 36
			#enemies_naval_strength_ratio > 0.85
			GER_enemy_blockade_north_sea = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = exasperation_mission_done
			}
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			set_country_flag = exasperation_mission_done
			activate_decision = GER_decision_naval_order_of_october_1918
			country_event = {
				id = ww1_germanrework.149
				days = 2
			}
		}
	}

			}

			has_country_flag = exasperation_mission_done
		}
		available = {
			has_war_with_major = yes
			#enemies_naval_strength_ratio > 0.85
			GER_enemy_blockade_north_sea = yes
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = {
			command_power > 9.9
		}
		custom_cost_text = decision_cost_CP_10
		remove_effect = {
			add_timed_idea = {
				idea = GER_all_or_nothing_plan
				days = 180
			}	
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_command_power = -10

				hidden_effect = {

				}
			}

				has_country_flag = kiel_mutiny
				has_war = no
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				date < 1918.08.20
			}
		}
	}

	# FAMINE TIMER
	#################
	GER_mission_famine_timer = {
		icon = GFX_decision_SWI_consolidate_council_power
		allowed = {
			original_tag = GER
		}
		days_mission_timeout = 550 #focus/decision will make it longer
		activation = {
			is_great_war_ongoing = yes
			has_idea = GER_food_shortages
			any_enemy_country = {
				NOT = {
					tag = ENG
					tag = SOV
				}
				is_major = yes
				has_capitulated = no
			}
			#enemies_naval_strength_ratio > 1.5
			GER_enemy_blockade_north_sea = yes
			OR = {
				USA = {
					has_war_with = PREV
					has_capitulated = no
				}
				ENG = {
					has_war_with = PREV
					has_capitulated = no
				}
			}
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = GER_capitulation
				is_in_faction_with = RUS
				has_idea = GER_famine
			}
		}
		available = {
			OR = {
				has_war = no
				has_global_flag = great_war_ends
				has_global_flag = GER_capitulation
				is_in_faction_with = RUS
				AND = {
					if = {
						limit = { 
							has_war_with = USA
						}
						USA = { has_capitulated = yes }
					}
					if = {
						limit = { 
							has_war_with = ENG
						}
						OR = {
							ENG = { has_capitulated = yes }
							#enemies_naval_strength_ratio < 1
							naval_dominance_north_sea = yes
						}					
					}
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			has_war = yes
			is_great_war_ongoing = yes
			NOT = {
				has_idea =  GER_famine
			}
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		is_good = no
		timeout_effect = {
			add_ideas = GER_famine
			hidden_effect = {
				country_event = {

	GER_add_submarines = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_merge_plant_ship
		allowed = {
			original_tag = GER
		}
		visible = {
			OR = {
				is_ai = yes
				AND = {
					is_debug = yes

				}

			}
			has_war = yes
			is_great_war_ongoing = yes
		}
		available = {
			# has_completed_focus = GER_atlantic_class
			has_completed_focus = GER_v_thirty_production

			has_capitulated = no
			has_war_with = ENG
			num_of_naval_factories > 10
			any_controlled_state = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				is_coastal = yes
			}
			has_tech = early_ship_hull_submarine
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		days_re_enable = 70
		complete_effect = {
			create_ship = {
				type = ship_hull_submarine_1
				creator = GER
				equipment_variant = "U Class"
			}
		}
		cost = 0
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 1000
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				# By 1917, it had 140 and the U-boats had destroyed about 30 percent of the world's merchant ships.
				has_navy_size = {
					size > 200
					type = submarine
				}
			}
		}
	}
	GER_stricten_rationing = {
		icon = generic_welfare
		days_remove = 50
		cost = 25
		allowed = {
			original_tag = GER
		}
		available = {
			has_completed_focus = GER_introduce_rationing_measures
		}
		visible = {
			has_completed_focus = GER_introduce_rationing_measures
			great_power_politics_great_war_visible = yes
			NOT = {
				has_idea = GER_idea_extreme_rationing
			}
			has_war_with_major = yes
			is_great_war_ongoing = yes
			OR = {
				has_idea = GER_famine
				has_idea = GER_food_shortages
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							has_country_flag = GER_1916_food_strikes
						}
					}
					country_event = {
						id = ww1_germanrework.155
						days = 3
					}
					set_country_flag = GER_1916_food_strikes
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = GER_idea_light_rationing
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = GER_idea_light_rationing
					add_idea = GER_idea_moderate_rationing
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = GER_mission_food_shortages_timer
					}
					add_days_mission_timeout = {
						mission = GER_mission_food_shortages_timer
						days = 50
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = GER_mission_famine_timer
					}
					add_days_mission_timeout = {
						mission = GER_mission_famine_timer
						days = 50
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = GER_idea_moderate_rationing
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = GER_idea_moderate_rationing
					add_idea = GER_idea_strict_rationing
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = GER_mission_food_shortages_timer
					}
					add_days_mission_timeout = {
						mission = GER_mission_food_shortages_timer
						days = 60
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = GER_mission_famine_timer
					}
					add_days_mission_timeout = {
						mission = GER_mission_famine_timer
						days = 60
					}
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					has_idea = GER_idea_strict_rationing
				}
				swap_ideas = {
					remove_idea = GER_idea_strict_rationing
					add_idea = GER_idea_extreme_rationing
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = GER_mission_food_shortages_timer
					}
					add_days_mission_timeout = {
						mission = GER_mission_food_shortages_timer
						days = 70
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = GER_mission_famine_timer
					}
					add_days_mission_timeout = {
						mission = GER_mission_famine_timer
						days = 70
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				has_country_flag = GER_1916_food_strikes
				factor = 0
			}
		}
	}

	before_the_leaves_fall = {
		icon = GFX_decision_eng_blackshirt_march
		allowed = {
			always = yes
		}
		days_mission_timeout = 75
		activation = {
			is_great_war_ongoing = yes
			GER = { has_war_with = FRA }
			NOT = {
				has_global_flag = GER_capitulation
				has_global_flag = ENT_capitulation
				has_global_flag = FRA_capitulation_alone
				has_global_flag = before_the_leaves_fall_flag
				has_global_flag = before_the_leaves_fall_failed
			}
		}
		available = {
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = before_the_leaves_fall_tt
				OR = {
					AND = {
						FRA = { has_war = no }
						GER = { has_war = no }
					}
					has_global_flag = great_war_ends
					has_global_flag = GER_capitulation
					has_global_flag = ENT_capitulation
					has_global_flag = FRA_capitulation_alone
					has_global_flag = before_the_leaves_fall_flag
					has_global_flag = before_the_leaves_fall_failed
				}
			}
		}
		visible = {
			is_great_war_ongoing = yes
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		is_good = no
		timeout_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				every_country = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_war_with = FRA
							has_war_with = GER
							has_war_together_with = GER
							has_war_together_with = FRA
						}
						NOT = { tag = GER }
					}
					add_war_support = -0.05
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					tag = GER
					NOT = { has_completed_focus = GER_heeringens_plan }
				}
				complete_national_focus = GER_schlieffen_plan_scuppered
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				set_global_flag = before_the_leaves_fall_failed
				if = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							has_war_with = FRA
							has_war_with = GER
							has_war_together_with = GER
							has_war_together_with = FRA
						}
						NOT = { tag = GER }
					}
					add_war_support = -0.05
				}
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			set_global_flag = before_the_leaves_fall_flag
		}
	}

		}
		remove_effect = {
			add_named_threat = {
				name = GER_decision_expand_mittelafrika
				threat = 1
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						is_owned_by = MAF
					}
					GER_is_MAF_state = yes
					OR = {
						is_coastal = yes
						any_neighbor_state = {
							is_owned_by = MAF
						}
					}
					owns_or_subject_of = yes
				}

				# If the country is a subject, and all its states are in Africa, completely annex it
				if = {
					limit = {
						owner = {
							# Don't know why this is needed, but got "MAF trying to annex itself" error without it sometimes, please enlighten me
							NOT = {
								tag = MAF
							}
							is_subject_of = ROOT
							all_owned_state = {
								GER_is_MAF_state = yes
							}
						}
					}
					owner = {
						drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
						every_unit_leader = { set_nationality = MAF }
						MAF = {
							annex_country = {
								target = PREV
								transfer_troops = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}

				transfer_state_to = MAF
				remove_claim_by = ROOT

				if = {
					limit = {
						THIS = {
							GER_is_MAF_core = yes
						}
					}
					add_core_of = MAF
				}
			}

			# Set capital to Dar es Salaam
			if = {
				limit = {
					MAF = {
						owns_state = 546
						546 = {
							is_capital = no
						}
					}
				}
				MAF = {
					set_capital = { state = 546 }
				}
			}

			hidden_effect = {
				MAF = {
					# What is the historical background of this bro
					every_owned_state = {
						limit = {
							OR = {
								is_core_of = GHA
								is_core_of = NGA
								is_core_of = KEN
							}
						}
						set_demilitarized_zone = yes
					}

					# Military access to Ethiopia to access Djibouti
					if = {
						limit = {
							ETH = { is_neighbor_of = PREV }
							GER = { owns_state = 268 }
						}
						ETH = {
							give_military_access = GER
							give_military_access = PREV
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								AFA = {
									exists = yes
									NOT = {
										has_war_with = GER
										has_war_with = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							AFA = {
								give_military_access = GER
								give_military_access = PREV
							}
						}
						if = {
							limit = {
								GOB = {
									exists = yes
									NOT = {
										has_war_with = GER
										has_war_with = PREV
									}
								}
							}
							GOB = {
								give_military_access = GER
								give_military_access = PREV
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

GER_interwar_reichspakt_category = {
	GER_interwar_invite_in_reichspakt = {
		icon = GFX_decision_eng_ally_imperialist_coup
		allowed = {
			OR = {
				original_tag = GER
				original_tag = AUS
			}
		}
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				NOT = {
					has_government = communism
					has_country_flag = great_war_loser
				}
				is_subject = no
				is_in_faction = no
				has_war = no
			}
		}
		targets = {
			BUL
			POL
			UKR
			IRE
			FIN
			EST
			LAT
			COU
			UBD
			LIT
			SCO			
		}
		target_root_trigger = {
			has_global_flag = frankfurt_treaty
			has_country_flag = great_war_winner
		}
		available = {
			FROM = {
				has_war = no							
			}		
		}
		visible = {
			OR = {
				AND = {
					original_tag = GER
					# has_completed_focus = GER_reichspakt
				}
				is_greater_germany = yes
			}
			NOT = {
				has_government = communism
			}
			has_global_flag = frankfurt_treaty
			has_country_flag = great_war_winner
			is_faction_leader = yes
		}
		fire_only_once = yes
		cost = 25
		complete_effect = {
			FROM = {
				hidden_effect = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_ai = yes
							OR = {
								tag = LIT
								tag = UBD
								tag = BLR
								tag = UKR
								tag = POL
								tag = FIN
							}
						}
						add_ai_strategy = {
							type = alliance
							id = ROOT
							value = 200
						}
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							is_historical_focus_on = no
							is_ai = yes
						}
						random_list = {
							4 = {
								add_ai_strategy = {
									type = alliance
									id = ROOT
									value = 200
								}
							}
							6 = {
							}
						}
					}
				}
				country_event = generic.5
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 85
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						tag = LIT
						tag = UBD
						tag = BLR
						tag = UKR
						tag = POL
						tag = FIN
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

GER_german_east_asia_cat = {
	GER_german_east_asia = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_operation
		allowed = { tag = GER }
		visible = {
			is_subject = no
			has_country_flag = great_war_winner
			NOT = { country_exists = OST }
			NOT = {
				has_government = communism
				has_country_flag = great_war_loser
			}
		}
		available = {
			has_war = no
			has_global_flag = frankfurt_treaty
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GER_control_OST_state_tt
				OR = {
					any_owned_state = {
						GER_is_OST_core = yes
					}
					has_subject = IND
				}
				
			}
		}
		cost = 100
		days_remove = 50
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				OR = {
					has_war = yes
					is_subject = yes
					NOT = {
						any_owned_state = {
							GER_is_OST_core = yes
						}
						has_subject = IND
					}
					has_government = communism
				}
			}
		}	
		cancel_effect = {
			add_political_power = 90
		}
		remove_effect = {
			GER_release_OST = yes
			custom_effect_tooltip = GER_release_OST
			country_event = ww1_germany.152
		}
	}
	GER_german_east_asia_expand = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_operation
		targets = {
			OST
		}
		target_root_trigger = {
			has_global_flag = frankfurt_treaty
		}
		allowed = { tag = GER }
		visible = {
			has_subject = OST
			is_subject = no
			any_state = {
				GER_is_OST_state = yes
				owns_or_subject_of = yes
				NOT = { is_owned_by = OST }
			}
			NOT = {
				has_government = communism
				has_country_flag = great_war_loser
			}
		}
		available = {
			has_war = no
			has_global_flag = frankfurt_treaty
			custom_override_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GER_control_OST_state_tt
				OR = {
					any_owned_state = {
						GER_is_OST_core = yes
					}
					has_subject = IND
				}
			}
		}
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				NOT = {
					is_owned_by = OST
				}
				GER_is_OST_state = yes
				owns_or_subject_of = yes
			}
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		cancel_trigger = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				OR = {
					has_war = yes
					is_subject = yes
					NOT = { has_subject = OST }
					has_government = communism
					NOT = {
						any_state = {
							GER_is_OST_state = yes
							owns_or_subject_of = yes
							NOT = { is_owned_by = OST }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			add_political_power = 25
		}
		cost = 30
		days_remove = 20
		days_re_enable = 50
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
		}
		remove_effect = {
			add_named_threat = {
				name = GER_german_east_asia_expand
				threat = 1
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						is_owned_by = OST
					}
					GER_is_OST_state = yes
					owns_or_subject_of = yes
				}

				# If the country is a subject, and all its states are Ostasien States, completely annex it
				owner = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = {
								tag = OST
							}
							is_subject_of = ROOT
							all_owned_state = {
								GER_is_OST_state = yes
							}
						}
						# Special stuff if the subject we are annexing is Indochina
						if = {
							limit = {
								tag = IND
							}
							if = {
								limit = {
									has_character = OST_wilhelm_filchner
								}
								OST_wilhelm_filchner = { set_nationality = OST }
							}
							OST = {
								OST_wilhelm_filchner = {
									promote_character = colonial_government
									promote_character = conservatism
								}
								if = {
									limit = {
										has_character = OST_maximilian_von_spee
									}
									OST_maximilian_von_spee = {
										promote_character = colonial_government
										promote_character = stratocracy
									}
								}
							}
						}
						else = {
							every_unit_leader = { set_nationality = OST }
						}

						# Annex the country
						drop_cosmetic_tag = yes
						OST = {
							annex_country = {
								target = PREV
								transfer_troops = yes
							}
						}
					}
				}

				transfer_state_to = OST
				remove_claim_by = ROOT

				if = {
					limit = {
						GER_is_OST_core = yes
					}
					add_core_of = OST
				}
			}
			
			# Try to set capital to Singapore
			OST = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						owns_state = 932
						932 = { is_capital = no }
					}
					set_capital = { state = 932 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

GER_bundesrat_category = {
	GER_start_test_voting = {
		available = {
			is_ai = no
		}
		visible = {
			is_debug = yes
			has_country_flag = GER_show_debug_decisions
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			GER_calculate_bundesrat_votes = yes
		}
		days_remove = 30
		remove_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_majority_in_bundesrat = yes }
				custom_effect_tooltip = GER_likely_to_pass
				#here should be effects of accepting this law, like apply country_flag and effects (stability, autonomy idk)
				hidden_effect = { country_event = ww1_german_reichstag.6 }
				else = { 
					custom_effect_tooltip = GER_likely_to_be_rejected
					hidden_effect = { country_event = ww1_german_reichstag.7 }
				}
			}
			GER_vote_ends = yes
		}
	}
	GER_sway_all_states= {
		available = {
			is_ai = no
		}
		visible = {
			is_debug = yes
			has_country_flag = GER_show_debug_decisions
			has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
		}
		complete_effect = {
			GER_sway_all_states = yes
		}
	}

	GER_call_in_prussia = {
		name = GER_call_in_FROM
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_political_discourse
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			NOT = { has_country_flag = GER_called_in_prussia }
			GER_is_prussia_available_in_bundesrat = yes
		}
		available = {
			# NOT = {
			# 	has_country_flag = GER_prussia_cooldown
			# }
			
		}
		on_map_mode = decision_view_only
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_claimed_by = PRE
				is_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = { 
			OR = {
				check_variable = { GER_extra_influence > 0 } 
				check_variable = { GER_influence_in_prussia > 0 } 
			}		
		}
		custom_cost_text = decision_cost_1_influence
		
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {check_variable = { GER_influence_in_prussia > 0 } }
				add_to_variable = { GER_influence_in_prussia = -1 }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { GER_extra_influence = -1 }
			}
			set_country_flag = GER_called_in_prussia
			set_temp_variable = { ger_modify_bundersrat_votes_in_favor_var = GER_votes_of_prussia }
			GER_modify_bundersrat_votes_in_favor = yes
			# custom_effect_tooltip = GER_prussia_cooldown_tt
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = GER_votes_of_prussia
			}
			modifier = {
				has_majority_in_bundesrat = yes
				factor = 0
			}
		}
	}

			}

	GER_call_in_free_cities = {
		name = GER_call_in_FROM
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_political_discourse
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			NOT = { has_country_flag = GER_called_in_free_cities }
			GER_is_any_free_city_available_in_bundesrat = yes
		}
		available = {
			# NOT = {
			# 	has_country_flag = GER_free_cities_cooldown
			# }
		}
		on_map_mode = decision_view_only
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				OR = {
					is_core_of = BRE
					is_core_of = HAM
					is_core_of = LUB
				}
				is_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		custom_cost_trigger = { 
			OR = {
				check_variable = { GER_extra_influence > 0 } 
				check_variable = { GER_influence_in_free_cities > 0 } 
			}
			
		}
		custom_cost_text = decision_cost_1_influence
		
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {check_variable = { GER_influence_in_free_cities > 0 } }
				add_to_variable = { GER_influence_in_free_cities = -1 }
			}
			else = {
				add_to_variable = { GER_extra_influence = -1 }
			}
			set_country_flag = GER_called_in_free_cities
			set_temp_variable = { ger_modify_bundersrat_votes_in_favor_var = GER_votes_of_free_cities }
			GER_modify_bundersrat_votes_in_favor = yes
			# custom_effect_tooltip = GER_free_cities_cooldown_tt
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = GER_votes_of_free_cities
			}
			modifier = {
				has_majority_in_bundesrat = yes
				factor = 0
			}
		}
	}

	GER_negotiations_with_prussia = {
		name = GER_negotiations_with_FROM
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_political_discourse
		visible = {
			GER_is_prussia_available_in_bundesrat = yes
			GER_is_prussia_loyal = no
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_prussia
				has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			}	
		}
		on_map_mode = decision_view_only
		fire_only_once = no
		days_re_enable = 1
		available = {	
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_federal_state
				has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			}	
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GER_not_investing_or_conceding_tt
				NOT = {				
					has_active_mission = GER_investment_in_prussia
					has_active_mission = GER_investment_in_prussia_infrastructure			
					any_owned_state = {
						is_claimed_by = PRE
						is_core_of = ROOT
						has_state_flag = GER_political_concession_target
					}
				}
			}		
		}
		#cost = 5
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT			
				is_claimed_by = PRE
				is_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_prussia
			set_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_federal_state
			country_event = ww1_germanrework.174
			custom_effect_tooltip = escape_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = GER_influence_points_count_in_prussia
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = GER_votes_of_prussia
			}
			modifier = {
				factor  = 0
				GER_ai_has_too_many_bundesrat_missions = yes
				GER_ai_has_too_many_bundesrat_infrastructure_missions = yes
				
			}
			modifier = {
				factor  = 0
				check_variable = { GER_influence_in_prussia > 2 }
			}
		}
	}
	GER_investment_in_prussia = {
		# name = GER_investment_in_FROM
		icon = generic_factory
		allowed = {
			tag = GER
		}
		available = {
			any_owned_state = {
				is_claimed_by = PRE
				has_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { is_ai = no } }
					custom_trigger_tooltip = {
						tooltip = tt_build_one_civ_trigger_generic
						check_variable = { 
							var = industrial_complex_level
							value = civilian_total_after_goal
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					has_state_flag = {
						flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
						days > 105
					}
				}
			}
		}
		activation = {
			always = no
		}
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_claimed_by = PRE
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				has_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
			}
		}
		days_mission_timeout = 365
		is_good = no
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				GER_is_prussia_available_in_bundesrat = no
				GER_is_prussia_loyal = yes
			}
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_claimed_by = PRE
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { GER_influence_in_prussia = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = GER_add_INFLUENCE_to_FEDERAL_STATE_tt
				INFLUENCE = "1"
				FEDERAL_STATE = GER_bundesrat_prussia_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_claimed_by = PRE
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			add_political_power = -50
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_claimed_by = PRE
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
	}

						tooltip = tt_build_one_civ_trigger_generic
						check_variable = { 
							var = industrial_complex_level
							value = civilian_total_after_goal
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					has_state_flag = {
						flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
						days > 105
					}
				}
			}
		}
		activation = {
			always = no
		}
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_core_of = SAX
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				has_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
			}
		}
		days_mission_timeout = 365
		is_good = no
		cancel_trigger = {
			GER_is_saxony_available_in_bundesrat = no
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = SAX
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { GER_influence_in_saxony = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = GER_add_INFLUENCE_to_FEDERAL_STATE_tt
				INFLUENCE = "1"
				FEDERAL_STATE = GER_bundesrat_saxony_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = SAX
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			add_political_power = -50
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = SAX
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
	}

	#Wurttemberg
	GER_negotiations_with_wurttemberg = {
		name = GER_negotiations_with_FROM
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_political_discourse
		visible = {
			GER_is_wurttemberg_available_in_bundesrat = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_wurttemberg
				has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			}
		}
		on_map_mode = decision_view_only
		fire_only_once = no
		days_re_enable = 1
		available = {	
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_federal_state
				has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			}	
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GER_not_investing_or_conceding_tt
				NOT = {				
					has_active_mission = GER_investment_in_wurttemberg
					has_active_mission = GER_investment_in_wurttemberg_infrastructure				
					any_owned_state = {
						is_core_of = WUR
						is_core_of = ROOT
						has_state_flag = GER_political_concession_target
					}
				}
			}		
		}
		#cost = 5
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = WUR
				is_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_wurttemberg
			set_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_federal_state
			country_event = ww1_germanrework.174
			custom_effect_tooltip = escape_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = GER_influence_points_count_in_wurttemberg
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = GER_votes_of_wurttemberg
			}
			modifier = {
				factor  = 0
				GER_ai_has_too_many_bundesrat_missions = yes
				GER_ai_has_too_many_bundesrat_infrastructure_missions = yes
				
			}
			modifier = {
				factor  = 0
				check_variable = { GER_influence_in_wurttemberg > 2 }
			}
		}
	}

				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			add_political_power = -50
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = HES
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}

	GER_investment_in_hesse_infrastructure = {
		# name = GER_investment_in_FROM_infrastructure
		icon = generic_railway
		allowed = {
			tag = GER
		}
		available = {
			any_owned_state = {
				is_core_of = HES
				has_state_flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { is_ai = no } }
					custom_trigger_tooltip = {
						tooltip = tt_build_one_infra_trigger_generic
						check_variable = { 
							var = infrastructure_level
							value = infrastructure_total_after_goal
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					has_state_flag = {
						flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
						days > 35
					}
				}
			}
		}
		activation = {
			always = no
		}
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_core_of = HES
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				has_state_flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
			}
		}
		days_mission_timeout = 180
		is_good = no
		cancel_trigger = {
			GER_is_hesse_available_in_bundesrat = no
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = HES
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { GER_influence_in_hesse = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = GER_add_INFLUENCE_to_FEDERAL_STATE_tt
				INFLUENCE = "1"
				FEDERAL_STATE =  GER_bundesrat_hesse_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = HES
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			add_political_power = -50
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = HES
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}

	#Mecklenburg
	GER_negotiations_with_mecklenburg = {
		name = GER_negotiations_with_FROM
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_political_discourse
		visible = {
			GER_is_hesse_available_in_bundesrat = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_mecklenburg
				has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			}
		}
		on_map_mode = decision_view_only
		fire_only_once = no
		days_re_enable = 1
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_federal_state
				has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			}	
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GER_not_investing_or_conceding_tt
				NOT = {				
					has_active_mission = GER_investment_in_mecklenburg
					has_active_mission = GER_investment_in_mecklenburg_infrastructure
					any_owned_state = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = MEK
							is_core_of = MEC
						}
						is_core_of = ROOT
						has_state_flag = GER_political_concession_target
					}
				}
			}
		}
		#cost = 5
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				OR = {
					is_core_of = MEK
					is_core_of = MEC
				}
				is_core_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_mecklenburg
			set_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_federal_state
			country_event = ww1_germanrework.174
			custom_effect_tooltip = escape_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = GER_influence_points_count_in_mecklenburg
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = GER_votes_of_mecklenburg
			}
			modifier = {
				factor  = 0
				GER_ai_has_too_many_bundesrat_missions = yes
				GER_ai_has_too_many_bundesrat_infrastructure_missions = yes
				
			}
			modifier = {
				factor  = 0
				check_variable = { GER_influence_in_mecklenburg > 2 }
			}
		}
	}

				if = {
					limit = { ROOT = { is_ai = no } }
					custom_trigger_tooltip = {
						tooltip = tt_build_one_infra_trigger_generic
						check_variable = { 
							var = infrastructure_level
							value = infrastructure_total_after_goal
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
				}
				else = {
					has_state_flag = {
						flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
						days > 35
					}
				}
			}
		}
		activation = {
			always = no
		}
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_core_of = ALS
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				has_state_flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
			}
		}
		days_mission_timeout = 180
		is_good = no
		cancel_trigger = {
			GER_is_alsace_lorraine_available_in_bundesrat = no
		}
		cancel_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = ALS
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		complete_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { GER_influence_in_alsace_lorraine = 1 }
			custom_effect_tooltip = {
				localization_key = GER_add_INFLUENCE_to_FEDERAL_STATE_tt
				INFLUENCE = "1"
				FEDERAL_STATE =  GER_bundesrat_alsace_lorraine_tt
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = ALS
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		timeout_effect = {
			add_political_power = -50
			hidden_effect = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						is_core_of = ALS
					}
					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_infrastructure
				}
			}
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
	}
	#Thuringia
	GER_negotiations_with_thuringia = {
		name = GER_negotiations_with_FROM
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_political_discourse
		visible = {
			GER_is_thuringia_available_in_bundesrat = yes
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_thuringia
				has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			}
		}
		on_map_mode = decision_view_only
		fire_only_once = no
		days_re_enable = 1
		available = {
			NOT = {
				has_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_federal_state
				has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat
			}	
			custom_trigger_tooltip = {
				tooltip = GER_not_investing_or_conceding_tt
				NOT = {				
					has_active_mission = GER_investment_in_thuringia
					has_active_mission = GER_investment_in_thuringia_infrastructure
					any_owned_state = {
						is_core_of = ROOT
						is_core_of = THU
						has_state_flag = GER_political_concession_target
					}
				}
			}
		}
		#cost = 5
		highlight_states = {
			highlight_states_trigger = {
				is_owned_by = ROOT
				is_core_of = ROOT
				is_core_of = THU
			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_thuringia
			set_country_flag = GER_negotiating_with_federal_state
			country_event = ww1_germanrework.174
			custom_effect_tooltip = escape_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = GER_influence_points_count_in_thuringia
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			base = 5
			modifier = {
				factor = GER_votes_of_thuringia
			}
			modifier = {
				factor  = 0
				GER_ai_has_too_many_bundesrat_missions = yes
				GER_ai_has_too_many_bundesrat_infrastructure_missions = yes
				
			}
			modifier = {
				factor  = 0
				check_variable = { GER_influence_in_thuringia > 2 }
			}
		}
	}
	GER_investment_in_thuringia = {
		# name = GER_investment_in_FROM
		icon = generic_factory
		allowed = {
			tag = GER
		}
		available = {
			any_owned_state = {
				is_core_of = ROOT
				is_core_of = THU

					limit = { ROOT = { is_ai = no } }

						}
					}
				}

					has_state_flag = {
						flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
						days > 105
					}
				}
			}
		}
		activation = {
			always = no

				is_core_of = THU
				is_core_of = ROOT
				is_owned_by = ROOT

					clr_state_flag = GER_building_mission_civilian_factory
				}
			}

		}

				has_country_flag = GER_vote_in_bundesrat

				tooltip = GER_not_investing_or_conceding_tt

						is_core_of = ROOT

						has_state_flag = GER_political_concession_target
					}
				}
			}
		}

			}

			custom_effect_tooltip = escape_line
			custom_effect_tooltip = GER_influence_points_count_in_austria
			#GER_update_bundesrat_gui = yes
		}

			modifier = {
				factor = GER_votes_of_austria
			}
			modifier = {
				factor  = 0
				GER_ai_has_too_many_bundesrat_missions = yes
				GER_ai_has_too_many_bundesrat_infrastructure_missions = yes
				
			}
			modifier = {
				factor  = 0
				check_variable = { GER_influence_in_austria > 2 }
			}
		}

GER_reichstag_category = {

	GER_toggle_debug_decisions = {
		available = {
			is_ai = no
		}
		fire_only_once = no
		visible = {
			is_debug = yes
		}
		complete_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = GER_show_debug_decisions
				}
				clr_country_flag = GER_show_debug_decisions
				else = {
					set_country_flag = GER_show_debug_decisions
				}
			}
		}
	}

	GER_debug_reload_parliament = {
		available = {
			is_ai = no
		}
		visible = {
			is_debug = yes
			has_country_flag = GER_show_debug_decisions
		}
		visible = {
			has_country_flag = GER_show_debug_decisions
		}

	GER_election_initial = {
		icon = generic_political_discourse
		desc = GER_election_desc

		is_good = yes
		fire_only_once = yes
		days_mission_timeout = 621 # 12/01/1912

		activation = {
			has_elections = yes
			has_start_date < 1914.1.1
		}
		available = {
			has_elections = no
		}
		cancel_trigger = {
			OR = {
				has_active_mission = GER_election2
				has_active_mission = GER_election_1919
			}
		}

		timeout_effect = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_war = no }
				GER = { 
					country_event = { 
						id = ww1_german_reichstag.1
					}
					country_event = {
						id = ww1_germanrework.45
						days = 10
					}
				}
				hidden_effect = {
					GER_reload_parliament_gui = yes
					GER_modify_parliament_seat = yes
				}
			}
			else = {
				country_event = ww1_german_reichstag.2
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				activate_mission = GER_election2
			}
		}
	}

					OR = {

						has_completed_focus = GER_new_constitution
					}
					NOT = {
						has_country_flag = GER_march_reforms_completed
					}
				}
			}
		}

			}
		}
		complete_effect = {
			set_country_flag = left_vote_in_progress
			set_country_flag = GER_reichstag_vote_in_progress
		}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_majority_in_reichstag = yes
				}
				add_political_power = 25
				add_stability = 0.02
				
				custom_effect_tooltip = GER_law_pass
				custom_effect_tooltip = GER_if_law_passes_reich
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_global_flag = GER_republic_proclaimed
					}
					GER_interwar_loser_new_constitution_effect = yes
					else = {
						GER_interwar_winner_new_constitution_effect = yes
					}
				}
				#hidden_effect = { country_event = ww1_german_reichstag.4 }
				else = {
					custom_effect_tooltip = GER_law_dont_pass
					custom_effect_tooltip = GER_if_law_passes_reich
					effect_tooltip = {
						if = {
							limit = {
								has_global_flag = GER_republic_proclaimed
							}
							GER_interwar_loser_new_constitution_effect = yes
							else = {
								GER_interwar_winner_new_constitution_effect = yes
							}
						}
					}
					hidden_effect = { country_event = ww1_german_reichstag.5 }
				}
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				GER_reset_sway_reichstag = yes
			}
		}
		days_remove = 21
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 50
		}

	GER_invite_from_in_mitteleuropa = {
		icon = GFX_decision_eng_trade_unions_support
		fire_only_once = no
		days_re_enable = 45
		cost = 10
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = {
				is_european_tag = yes
				is_microstate = no
				NOT = {
					has_idea = closed_economy
					original_tag = ENG
					is_in_faction_with = ENG
					has_government = communism
					has_idea = GER_mitteleuropa_member
					has_country_flag = GER_refused_mitteleuropa
				}
				if = {
					limit = { is_subject = yes }
					OVERLORD = { has_idea = GER_mitteleuropa_member }
				}
			}
		}
		allowed = {
			tag = GER
		}
		available = {
			has_war = no
			is_subject = no
		}
		complete_effect = {
			FROM = {
				country_event = {
					id = ww1_german_victory.25
					random_days = 3
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = {
			factor = 100
			modifier = {
				FROM = {
					OR = {
						has_country_flag = great_war_loser
						original_tag = FRA
						has_opinion = {
							target = FROM
							value < -25
						}
					}			
				}
				factor = 0
			}
		}
	}
